# Agents · D · part 15

951 entries, design-reviewer to designer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **design-reviewer**: Specialist reviewer for UI/UX — responsive layout, design-system consistency, accessibility (WCAG 2.1 AA), and interaction states. Use proactively when changes touch components, pages, styles, or markup. Returns a structured findings BRIEF — never modifies code, never changes task status. ([Elad73/agentic-base-layer](https://github.com/Elad73/agentic-base-layer/blob/HEAD/global/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Read-only review of a diff against the principle skills and their applicability and precedence guidance. Answers only "does this follow our principles". Runs in parallel with the validator and security-reviewer. ([eng-rodriguez/software-factory](https://github.com/eng-rodriguez/software-factory/blob/HEAD/plugins/factory/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Compara visualmente uma tela construída com o protótipo do engenheiro (docs/design/capturas) usando o checklist de .claude/rules/frontend-design.md. Somente leitura no código; devolve relatório de falhas de design para o builder. Use no ciclo-tela depois do tester verde. ([euguilhermegirardi/khazobras](https://github.com/euguilhermegirardi/khazobras/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Visual UI review in a real browser. Use PROACTIVELY after building or changing any user-facing UI (pages, components, layout, styling) to catch visual and design-quality regressions. Launches the app, drives it with Playwright, captures screenshots, evaluates them against the project's design skills, and reports concrete deviations with screenshot evidence. Reports only — it does not edit code. ([ezmartinez-conexa/Project.Brain-Metric.Induction](https://github.com/ezmartinez-conexa/Project.Brain-Metric.Induction/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Read-only UI/visual design auditor. ([SalvageUnion-io/SU-SRD](https://github.com/SalvageUnion-io/SU-SRD/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Independent, adversarial visual reviewer for UI work. Use after any page or screen is built or restyled, before reporting done. Judges rendered screenshots cold (never sees the builder's reasoning) on desirability first (is it beautiful, would the client love it?), then craft and distinctiveness, and returns SHIP / FIX FIRST / REWORK with scores and concrete fixes. ([sam-pop/agency-of-one](https://github.com/sam-pop/agency-of-one/blob/HEAD/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: 画面・LP・スライドなど見た目のある成果物を検収する読み取り専用のレビュアー。standards/quality.md のデザイン5項目を PASS / FAIL で判定する。スクリーンショットやHTML/CSSを読んで判断し、自分では直さない。 ([sammyTI/dotcompany-template](https://github.com/sammyTI/dotcompany-template/blob/HEAD/plugins/company/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Review product or UI plans, frontend code changes, or design specs for visual hierarchy, accessibility, interaction quality, empty/error states, and UI consistency. Use this agent when the user wants design critique on a screen, component, flow, or frontend diff before it ships. ([sanjaesuresh/s-suite](https://github.com/sanjaesuresh/s-suite/blob/HEAD/global/agents/design-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **design-reviewer**: READ-ONLY review that pages match the editorial × terminal design system. Use when a new page/component is added or restyled and you want to confirm it uses the design tokens, fonts, shared shell, the md·json·mcp chip, and respects reduced-motion. Reports deviations; never edits. ([satyajitghana/ai](https://github.com/satyajitghana/ai/blob/HEAD/brand-crew/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Decides whether a design or an ADR decision is sound enough to build on; the design transition. ([Screenfields/ccplugin-alfred-agent-workflow](https://github.com/Screenfields/ccplugin-alfred-agent-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Reviews the RENDERED result of UI work, not the diff. Comparative against sibling screens and the design system. Blocking only on the deterministic subset; advisory on taste, escalating to a human design authority. Runs on webapp-variant stories after Rev. ([sdk451/claude-gdrive](https://github.com/sdk451/claude-gdrive/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Design reviewer. Reviews UX, visual quality and accessibility from screenshots. Use for UI changes. ([seancabalse/agentic-playbook](https://github.com/seancabalse/agentic-playbook/blob/HEAD/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Compares a built screen with its design or direction and lists every visible difference. ([sebi1216/LOOPLIFT](https://github.com/sebi1216/LOOPLIFT/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Independent, read-only review of a project's UX and UI design (DESIGN.md) and, when code exists, of the interface code. Finds journeys that miss requirements, missing states, excess steps, inconsistent vocabulary, weak error messages, accessibility problems, token and component inconsistencies and deviations from platform conventions. It reports, it never edits. Use after the `ux` or `ui` skills, or whenever a skeptical design critique is wanted before building screens. ([sebmafate/trellis](https://github.com/sebmafate/trellis/blob/HEAD/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: 설계 파이프라인의 최종 게이트키퍼. Requirement Analyst → Codebase Explorer → Architect의 결과를 입력받아 3관점(고객/프로젝트/성능) + 체크리스트로 검증하고, 기술 결정마다 이유·트레이드오프를 개발자와 함께 확정한다. 최종 검증 통과 시에만 설계 문서 MD 파일을 생성한다. ([SGMRT/sgmrt-server](https://github.com/SGMRT/sgmrt-server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-reviewer.md))
- **design-reviewer**: Independent, read-only design reviewer. Never edits design artifacts; returns APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NON_BLOCKING_FINDINGS | CHANGES_REQUIRED | HUMAN_DECISION_REQUIRED. - **design-verifier**: Verifies a product plan (scope, completion criteria, Non-Goals, priority, issue grouping/closure) or a design document (user flows, contracts, data models, security boundaries, delegation briefs) before implementation. Read-only; returns BLOCKER/NOTE findings with evidence. Effort high by default; the caller passes xhigh per call when several decisions interlock or a mistake is expensive to reverse (contracts, data models, new flows, installer-disk paths, multi-milestone plans), and asks the user before max (auth, permissions, deletion, data export, money, product scope). - **design-verifier**: Use PROACTIVELY before claiming design work is complete. Blocks merge if any check fails. Produces a verification report with screenshots + scores attached to the PR.
